# (via https://github.com/daftblazer/bustin-out-decomp reference/sims2_symbols.txt, from
# https://github.com/natebag/Sims2DECOMP). Paired class by class by identical function size and
# order, with the Bustin' Out decomp's rules (tools/engine_names.py): runs of 3+ pairs with 3
# distinct sizes, two over 0x10, whose size sequence occurs only once in our ProDG code.
# These are hints, not renames: the names are Sims 2's readable forms (Class::Method_args), not
# GCC-mangled symbols, and the method bodies may differ between the games.
